
===== admin/dashboard/getDashboardData =====
--- dashboard.html:1853
 1838: 
 1839:     function formatINR(num) {
 1840:         return '₹' + num.toLocaleString('en-IN', {
 1841:             minimumFractionDigits: 2,
 1842:             maximumFractionDigits: 2
 1843:         });
 1844:     }
 1845: 
 1846:     function fetchDashboardData() {
 1847:         // Show loading animation before fetch
 1848:         $("#credit_limit_value, #balance_value, #total_balance_value, #winnings_value")
 1849:             .fadeTo(200, 0.5)
 1850:             .text("Loading...");
 1851: 
 1852:         $.ajax({
 1853:             url: "https://admin.99-exch.red/admin/dashboard/getDashboardData",
 1854:             type: "GET",
 1855:             dataType: "json",
 1856:             cache: false,
 1857:             success: function(data) {
 1858:                 console.log("Dashboard AJAX:", data);
 1859: 
 1860:                 // Clean numbers from API response
 1861:                 const creditLimit = cleanNumber(data.credit_limit);
 1862:                 const balance = cleanNumber(data.balance);
 1863:                 const totalBalance = cleanNumber(data.total_balance);
 1864:                 const winnings = cleanNumber(data.winnings);
 1865: 
 1866:                 // Animate and update values
 1867:                 $("#credit_limit_value").fadeTo(200, 0.3, function() {
 1868:                     // Credit limit → plain number (no ₹)
 1869:                     $(this).text(creditLimit.toLocaleString('en-IN', {
 1870:                         minimumFractionDigits: 0
 1871:                     })).fadeTo(200, 1);
 1872:                 });
 1873: 
 1874:                 $("#balance_value").fadeTo(200, 0.3, function() {
 1875:                     // Balance → ₹ prefixed
 1876:                     $(this).text(formatINR(balance)).fadeTo(200, 1);
 1877:                 });
 1878: 
 1879:                 $("#total_balance_value").fadeTo(200, 0.3, function() {
 1880:                     // Total balance → ₹ prefixed
 1881:                     $(this).text(formatINR(totalBalance)).fadeTo(200, 1);
 1882:                 });
 1883:

 1967: 
 1968:     // AJAX to your existing controller method
 1969:     function fetchSummaryByRange(fDate, tDate) {
 1970:         if (!fDate || !tDate) return;
 1971: 
 1972:         $.ajax({
 1973:             url: "https://admin.99-exch.red/admin/dashboard/getDateWiseTransactionSummary",
 1974:             type: "POST",
 1975:             dataType: "json",
 1976:             data: {
 1977:                 fdate: fDate,
 1978:                 tdate: tDate
 1979:             },
 1980:             success: function(res) {
 1981:                 // If server echoes JSON as string, ensure parse
 1982:                 const data = (typeof res === 'string') ? JSON.parse(res) : res;
 1983:                 updateSummaryUI(data);
 1984:             },
 1985:             error: function(xhr) {
 1986:                 console.error('Summary fetch error', xhr?.responseText || xhr.statusText);
 1987:             }
 1988:         });
 1989:     }
 1990:

 2000:             day: '2-digit',
 2001:             month: 'short'
 2002:         });

--- dashboard.html:2178
 2163:             fetchSummaryByRange(toISO(f), toISO(t));
 2164:         });

 2167: <script>
 2168:     function formatINR(amount) {
 2169:         // Round down to integer
 2170:         const rounded = Math.floor(amount);
 2171:         return '₹' + rounded.toLocaleString('en-IN');
 2172:     }
 2173: 
 2174:     function fetchDownlineBalance() {
 2175:         $("#downline_balance_value").fadeTo(200, 0.5).text("Loading...");
 2176: 
 2177:         $.ajax({
 2178:             url: "https://admin.99-exch.red/admin/dashboard/getDownlineBalance",
 2179:             type: "GET",
 2180:             dataType: "json",
 2181:             cache: false,
 2182:             success: function(data) {
 2183:                 const balance = parseFloat(data.downline_balance);
 2184: 
 2185:                 $("#downline_balance_value").fadeTo(200, 0.3, function() {
 2186:                     $(this).text(formatINR(balance)).fadeTo(200, 1);
 2187:                 });
 2188:             },
 2189:             error: function(xhr, status, error) {
 2190:                 console.error("Downline Balance AJAX Error:", error);
 2191:             }
 2192:         });
 2193:     }
 2194: 
 2195:     // Initial load
 2196:     fetchDownlineBalance();
 2197: 
 2198:     // Refresh every 10 seconds
 2199:     setInterval(fetchDownlineBalance, 50000);
 2200: </script>      <!-- <script type="text/javascript">

 2203:     window.onpopstate = function () {
 2204:         history.go(1);
 2205:     };
 2206: </script> -->

--- all-deposit-requests (1).html:989
  974:             preConfirm: (remark) => {
  975:                 if (!remark) {
  976:                     Swal.showValidationMessage('Please enter remarks before rejecting');
  977:                     return false;
  978:                 }
  979:                 return remark;
  980:             }
  981:         }).then((result) => {
  982:             if (result.isConfirmed) {
  983:                 var remarks = result.value; // <-- get remarks from SweetAlert input
  984: 
  985:                 confirmButton.disabled = true;
  986:                 rejectButton.disabled = true;
  987: 
  988:                 $.ajax({
  989:                     url: `https://admin.99-exch.red/change-deposit-request-status-ajax/${status}/${id}/${remarks}`,
  990:                     type: "POST",
  991:                     dataType: 'json',
  992:                     success: function (data) {
  993:                         console.log('data', data);
  994: 
  995:                         if (data.status) {
  996:                             new PNotify({
  997:                                 title: "Success",
  998:                                 text: data.message,
  999:                                 styling: "bootstrap3",
 1000:                                 type: "success",
 1001:                                 delay: 3000,
 1002:                             });
 1003: 
 1004:                             // ✅ Remove row from table
 1005:                             // $(`#row_${id}`).remove();
 1006: 
 1007:                             setTimeout(function () {
 1008:                                 location.reload();
 1009:                             }, 2000);
